[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to find it and prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at an answer that settles less than the question asks (a partial or conditional answer, "not known") is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it means showing that the answer is right in every case the question covers, and that no other answer is. Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

```
from pathlib import Path
code=r'''"""Independent finite checks for low-degree facts used in the Andreadakis problem.
Run with Python. Uses exact integer/rational arithmetic only.
These tests corroborate, and do not replace, the general mathematical arguments.
"""
from itertools import product
from math import prod
import sympy as S

def add(a,b,scale=1):
    c=dict(a)
    for w,v in b.items():
        c[w]=c.get(w,0)+scale*v
        if not c[w]: del c[w]
    return c

def mul(a,b,N):
    c={}
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=N:
                w=u+v;c[w]=c.get(w,0)+x*y
    return {w:v for w,v in c.items() if v}

def bracket(a,b):
    N=max(map(len,a),default=0)+max(map(len,b),default=0)
    return add(mul(a,b,N),mul(b,a,N),-1)

def magnus(word,N):
    a={():1}
    for k in word:
        i=abs(k)-1
        b={():1,(i,):1} if k>0 else {tuple([i]*d):(-1)**d for d in range(N+1)}
        a=mul(a,b,N)
    return a

def inverse(word):return tuple(-x for x in word[::-1])
def comm(u,v):return tuple(u)+tuple(v)+inverse(u)+inverse(v)

def johnson1(image,i):
    a=magnus(tuple(image)+(-i,),2)
    return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if len(w)==2}

def check_magnus_generators(n):
    # Basis coordinate is (input, ordered pair a<b).
    labels=[(i,a,b) for i in range(1,n+1) for a in range(1,n+1) for b in range(a+1,n+1)]
    columns=[]
    for i in range(1,n+1):
        for j in range(1,n+1):
            if j==i:continue
            im=(j,i,-j)
            d=johnson1(im,i)
            columns.append([d.get((a-1,b-1),0) if k==i else 0 for k,a,b in labels])
        for j in range(1,n+1):
            for k in range(j+1,n+1):
                if i in (j,k):continue
                im=(i,)+comm((j,),(k,))
                d=johnson1(im,i)
                columns.append([d.get((a-1,b-1),0) if q==i else 0 for q,a,b in labels])
    M=S.Matrix.hstack(*(S.Matrix(c) for c in columns))
    assert M.rows==M.cols==n*n*(n-1)//2
    # In fact a signed permutation matrix, so no expensive determinant needed.
    assert all(sum(abs(M[i,j]) for i in range(M.rows))==1 for j in range(M.cols))
    assert all(sum(abs(M[i,j]) for j in range(M.cols))==1 for i in range(M.rows))
    return M.rows

def lyndon(w):return bool(w) and all(w<w[i:] for i in range(1,len(w)))

def lie_word(w):
    assert lyndon(w)
    if len(w)==1:return {w:1}
    for i in range(1,len(w)):
        if lyndon(w[i:]):
            return bracket(lie_word(w[:i]),lie_word(w[i:]))
    raise AssertionError

def check_center(n,d):
    W=[w for w in product(range(n),repeat=d) if lyndon(w)]
    bases=[lie_word(w) for w in W]
    witt=sum(int(S.mobius(k))*n**(d//k) for k in S.divisors(d))//d
    assert len(W)==witt
    rows=[(i,w) for i in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=d+1)]
    cols=[]
    for b in bases:
        images=[bracket(b,{(i,):1}) for i in range(n)]
        cols.append([images[i].get(w,0) for i,w in rows])
    A=S.Matrix.hstack(*(S.Matrix(v) for v in cols))
    assert A.rank()==len(W)
    return len(W)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for n in range(2,8):
        print('Johnson1 signed basis',n,check_magnus_generators(n))
    for n,maxdegree in [(2,7),(3,4)]:
        for d in range(1,maxdegree+1):
            print('zero free-Lie center',n,d,check_center(n,d))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_low_degree_checks.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_low_degree_checks.py','exec'))

```

Path('workspace/agent2_low_degree_proof.txt').write_text('''VERIFIED EXCLUSIONS\n(1) For every n>=2, (n,1) is not in D.\nMagnus generators for IA_n are K_ij (xi -> xj xi xj^-1) and K_i;jk (xi -> xi[xj,xk], j<k distinct from i), fixing the other basis elements. Their number is N=n^2(n-1)/2. The degree-one Johnson map tau: IA_n -> Hom(H,L_2(H)) sends these generators to a signed Z-basis. Consequently the surjection Z^N -> IA_n^ab induced by this generating set is an isomorphism: its composite with tau_ab is a signed permutation isomorphism. Thus ker(tau)=gamma_2(IA_n). By the definition of tau its kernel is A_2(n), proving the assertion.\n\n(2) For every c>=1, (2,c) is not in D.\nNielsen's theorem gives IA_2=Inn(F_2), and F_2 has trivial center. Thus gamma_r(IA_2)=Inn(gamma_r(F_2)). The center of F_2/gamma_{r+1}(F_2) is gamma_r/gamma_{r+1}. To see this, if a central class has a representative with first nonzero lower-central homogeneous term P of degree d<r, the Magnus-Witt embedding of the free Lie algebra into the tensor algebra implies [P,X_i]=0 for every basis letter X_i. The centralizer of X_i in the free associative algebra is Z[X_i], and the intersections for two distinct letters consist of scalars. Thus P=0, contradiction. (The centralizer assertion follows by comparing coefficients in PX_i=X_iP: any monomial not a power of X_i would generate infinitely many distinct monomials with the same nonzero coefficient, impossible for a polynomial.) Hence an inner automorphism by w belongs to A_r iff w is in gamma_r(F_2), proving A_r(2)=gamma_r(IA_2). Consequently the intersection defining D equals gamma_{c+1} for rank2.\n\nCOMPUTATIONAL CHECKS\nThe companion file agent2_low_degree_checks.py uses actual degree-two Magnus expansions to check the signed-basis assertion for 2<=n<=7. It checks the zero-center free-Lie assertion by exact rational ranks for n=2,1<=degree<=7 and n=3,1<=degree<=4. Output signed-basis dimensions 2,9,24,50,90,147; Lie dimensions rank2 2,1,2,3,6,9,18 and rank3 3,3,8,18.\n''')

To see this, if a central class has a representative with first nonzero lower-central homogeneous term P of degree d<r, the Magnus-Witt embedding of the free Lie algebra into the tensor algebra implies [P,X_i]=0 for every basis letter X_i. The centralizer of X_i in the free associative algebra is Z[X_i], and the intersections for two distinct letters consist of scalars. Thus P=0, contradiction. from pathlib import Path
code=r'''from itertools import combinations
from collections import Counter
from math import gcd
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form, hermite_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ

def red(w):
    st=[]
    for x in w:
        if st and st[-1]==-x:st.pop()
        else:st.append(x)
    return tuple(st)
def inv(w):return tuple(-i for i in w[::-1])
def cat(*words):return red(x for w in words for x in w)
def cm(a,b):return cat(a,b,inv(a),inv(b))
def generators(n):
    out=[]
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i!=j:out.append(('C',i,j))
        for j,k in combinations(range(n),2):
            if i not in (j,k):out.append(('M',i,j,k))
    return out

def extend(A,g,s):
    typ,i,*args=g
    B=list(A)
    if typ=='C':
        j=args[0]; v=A[j] if s==1 else inv(A[j]);B[i]=cat(v,A[i],inv(v))
    else:
        j,k=args;v=cm(A[j],A[k]);B[i]=cat(A[i],v if s==1 else inv(v))
    return tuple(B)

def lattice_relations(n=3,depth=4):
    G=generators(n);N=len(G)
    pairs=list(combinations(range(N),2));pidx={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)};M=len(pairs)
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n))
    z=(0,)*N; zz=(0,)*M
    seen={unit:(z,zz,())}; layer=[(unit,z,zz,())]
    rels={}; found=Counter()
    for d in range(1,depth+1):
        nxt=[]
        for A,e,c,w in layer:
            for a,g in enumerate(G):
                for s in (1,-1):
                    letter=s*(a+1)
                    if w and w[-1]==-letter:continue
                    B=extend(A,g,s)
                    f=list(e); f[a]+=s; f=tuple(f)
                    v=list(c)
                    for j in range(a+1,N):v[pidx[a,j]]-=s*e[j]
                    v=tuple(v); ww=w+(letter,)
                    if B in seen:
                        f0,v0,w0=seen[B]
                        assert f0==f
                        rel=tuple(x-y for x,y in zip(v,v0))
                        if any(rel):
                            if next(x for x in rel if x)<0:rel=tuple(-x for x in rel); ww,w0=w0,ww
                            if rel not in rels:rels[rel]=(ww,w0);found[d]+=1
                    else:
                        seen[B]=(f,v,ww);nxt.append((B,f,v,ww))
        layer=nxt
        R=S.Matrix(list(rels)).T if rels else S.zeros(M,0)
        print('depth',d,'seen',len(seen),'layer',len(layer),'relations',len(rels),'rank',R.rank(),flush=True)
    R=S.Matrix(list(rels)).T
    H=hermite_normal_form(R)
    D=smith_normal_form(H,domain=ZZ)
    elementary=[D[i,i] for i in range(min(D.shape))]
    print('relation rank',H.cols,'Smith invariants',elementary,flush=True)
    return G,pairs,rels,H

if __name__=='__main__':
    import pickle
    G,pairs,rels,H=lattice_relations()
    with open('workspace/agent2_ia3_quadratic_relations.pkl','wb') as f:pickle.dump((G,pairs,rels,H),f)
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_source_relations.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_source_relations.py','exec'))

```

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import hermite_normal_form,smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from itertools import combinations

def standard(n):return [(i,j,k) for i in range(n) for j,k in combinations(range(n),2)]
def coords(n):
    labels=standard(n); inds={a:i for i,a in enumerate(labels)};G=generators(n); N=len(G)
    J=S.zeros(N,N)
    for col,g in enumerate(G):
        if g[0]=='C':
            _,i,j=g;a,b=sorted((i,j));sign=1 if j<i else -1
        else:_,i,a,b=g;sign=1
        J[inds[i,a,b],col]=sign
    assert J.T*J==S.eye(N)
    return J

def gl_action(n,A):
    L=standard(n);N=len(L);Ai=A.inv();T=S.zeros(N,N)
    for c,(i,a,b) in enumerate(L):
        for r,(q,j,k) in enumerate(L):
            T[r,c]=Ai[i,q]*(A[j,a]*A[k,b]-A[k,a]*A[j,b])
    J=coords(n)
    return J.T*T*J

def wedge2(T):
    P=list(combinations(range(T.rows),2));return S.Matrix([[T[i,a]*T[j,b]-T[i,b]*T[j,a] for a,b in P] for i,j in P])

def close_GL(n,H):
    actions=[]
    for a in range(n):
        for b in range(n):
            if a!=b:
                A=S.eye(n);A[a,b]=1;actions.append(wedge2(gl_action(n,A)))
    for it in range(10):
        B=hermite_normal_form(S.Matrix.hstack(H,*(T*H for T in actions)))
        print('closure',it,'shape',B.shape,flush=True)
        if B==H:break
        H=B
    else:raise Exception('not stable')
    D=smith_normal_form(H,domain=ZZ)
    ds=[D[i,i] for i in range(H.cols)]
    print('Smith',ds,flush=True)
    return H

if __name__=='__main__':
    G,P,R,H=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_quadratic_relations.pkl','rb'))
    B=close_GL(3,H)
    pickle.dump(B,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_GL_relations.pkl','wb'))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_gl_closure.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_gl_closure.py','exec'))

new_string:
```
def bracket_matrix(n):
    from agent2_low_degree_checks import add,bracket
    L=standard(n);J=coords(n);G=generators(n);N=len(G)
    ds=[]
    for c in range(N):
        D=[{} for i in range(n)]
        for r,(i,j,k) in enumerate(L):
            if J[r,c]:D[i]=add(D[i],bracket({(j,):1},{(k,):1}),int(J[r,c]))
        ds.append(D)
    def apply(D,w):
        ans={}
        for a in range(len(w)):
            for v,c in D[w[a]].items():
                u=w[:a]+v+w[a+1:];ans[u]=ans.get(u,0)+c
        return ans
    from itertools import product
    rows=[(i,w) for i in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)]
    columns=[]
    for a,b in combinations(range(N),2):
        E=[{} for i in range(n)]
        for i in range(n):
            for w,c in ds[b][i].items():E[i]=add(E[i],apply(ds[a],w),c)
            for w,c in ds[a][i].items():E[i]=add(E[i],apply(ds[b],w),-c)
        columns.append([E[i].get(w,0) for i,w in rows])
    return S.Matrix.hstack(*(S.Matrix(c) for c in columns))

if __name__=='__main__':
    G,P,R,H=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_quadratic_relations.pkl','rb'))
    B=close_GL(3,H)
    J=bracket_matrix(3)
    assert J*B==S.zeros(J.rows,B.cols)
    assert J.rank()==18 and B.cols==18
    print('Verified primitive relation lattice equals degree2 Johnson kernel',flush=True)
    pickle.dump(B,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_GL_relations.pkl','wb'))
```

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend
from agent2_gl_closure import gl_action
from itertools import combinations
import sympy as S

class UnitLattice:
    def __init__(self):self.b={};self.queue=[];self.blocked=0
    def insert(self,v):
        v={i:int(c) for i,c in v.items() if c}
        while v:
            p=min(v);a=v[p]
            if p in self.b:
                w=self.b[p]
                for i,c in w.items():
                    t=v.get(i,0)-a*c
                    if t:v[i]=t
                    else:v.pop(i,None)
            else:
                if abs(a)!=1:self.blocked+=1;return False
                if a<0:v={i:-c for i,c in v.items()}
                self.b[p]=v;self.queue.append(v);return True
        return False

def action_maps(n):
    N=len(generators(n));P=list(combinations(range(N),2));pi={x:i for i,x in enumerate(P)}
    out=[]
    for a in range(n):
        for b in range(n):
            if a==b:continue
            A=S.eye(n);A[a,b]=1;T=gl_action(n,A)
            cols=[[(i,int(T[i,j])) for i in range(N) if T[i,j]] for j in range(N)]
            act=[]
            for i,j in P:
                v={}
                for a,x in cols[i]:
                    for b,y in cols[j]:
                        if a==b:continue
                        k=pi[min(a,b),max(a,b)];s=1 if a<b else -1
                        v[k]=v.get(k,0)+s*x*y
                act.append({k:c for k,c in v.items() if c})
            out.append(act)
    return out

def apply(act,v):
    ans={}
    for i,c in v.items():
        for j,d in act[i].items():
            t=ans.get(j,0)+c*d
            if t:ans[j]=t
            else:ans.pop(j,None)
    return ans

def seed(n,L):
    G=generators(n);gi={g:i for i,g in enumerate(G)};P=list(combinations(range(len(G)),2));pi={p:i for i,p in enumerate(P)}
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n))
    for k,(i,j) in enumerate(P):
        if extend(extend(unit,G[i],1),G[j],1)==extend(extend(unit,G[j],1),G[i],1):L.insert({k:1})
    print('commuting rank',len(L.b),flush=True)
    G4,P4,R4,H4=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia4_quadratic_relations.pkl','rb'))
    H4=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia4_GL_relations.pkl','rb'))
    cols=[{i:int(H4[i,j]) for i in range(H4.rows) if H4[i,j]} for j in range(H4.cols)]
    for subset in combinations(range(n),4):
        ids=[]
        for g in G4:ids.append(gi[(g[0],)+tuple(subset[x] for x in g[1:])])
        emb={i:(pi[min(ids[a],ids[b]),max(ids[a],ids[b])],1 if ids[a]<ids[b] else -1) for i,(a,b) in enumerate(P4)}
        for col in cols:L.insert({emb[i][0]:c*emb[i][1] for i,c in col.items()})
    print('seed rank',len(L.b),'blocked',L.blocked,flush=True)

def run(n):
    start=time.time();L=UnitLattice();seed(n,L)
    acts=action_maps(n);print('actions ready',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    idx=0
    while idx<len(L.queue):
        v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
        for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
        if idx%500==0:print('processed',idx,'rank',len(L.b),'blocked',L.blocked,'secs',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    N=n*n*(n-1)//2;target=N*(N-1)//2-(n*n*(n*n-1)//3-n*(n+1)//2)
    print('FINAL',n,'rank',len(L.b),'expected kernel',target,'blocked',L.blocked,'seconds',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    assert len(L.b)==target
    pickle.dump(L.b,open(f'workspace/agent2_ia{n}_sparse_relations.pkl','wb'))
    return L
if __name__=='__main__':run(6)
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_sparse_closure.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_sparse_closure.py','exec'))

def johnson_columns(n):
    G=generators(n);ds=[]
    for g in G:
        if g[0]=='C':_,i,j=g;ds.append((i,{(j,i):1,(i,j):-1}))
        else:_,i,j,k=g;ds.append((i,{(j,k):1,(k,j):-1}))
    def act(D,w):
        r,P=D;ans={}
        for a,x in enumerate(w):
            if x==r:
                for v,c in P.items():
                    u=w[:a]+v+w[a+1:];ans[u]=ans.get(u,0)+c
        return ans
    cols=[]
    for a,b in combinations(range(len(G)),2):
        v={}
        for D,E,s in [(ds[a],ds[b],1),(ds[b],ds[a],-1)]:
            root,P=E
            for w,c in P.items():
                for u,d in act(D,w).items():
                    idx=root*n**3+u[0]*n*n+u[1]*n+u[2]
                    v[idx]=v.get(idx,0)+s*c*d
        cols.append({i:c for i,c in v.items() if c})
    return cols

def certify(n,L):
    cols=johnson_columns(n);I=UnitLattice()
    for c in cols:I.insert(c)
    N=n*n*(n-1)//2
    assert len(I.b)+len(L.b)==N*(N-1)//2
    for rel in L.b.values():
        image={}
        for i,c in rel.items():
            for j,d in cols[i].items():image[j]=image.get(j,0)+c*d
        assert not any(image.values())
    print('CERTIFIED',n,'relation rank',len(L.b),'Johnson rank',len(I.b),'source quotient injects integrally',flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for n in [5,6]:certify(n,run(n))

from pathlib import Path
text=r'''# Integral injectivity in degree 2, all ranks

Let H=Z^n, V=H^* tensor Lambda^2 H, Q=Lambda^2 V, T=H^* tensor L_3(H), and B:Q->T be derivation bracket. Magnus generation and the first Johnson map identify IA_n^ab=V. Consequently the true relation lattice R_n=ker(Q->gr^gamma_2 IA_n) lies in ker B. Any actual group relation with zero exponent sums yields an element of R_n by taking its class-two free-group signature, and R_n is stable under GL_n(Z).

## Exact finite certificates
`agent2_source_relations.py` enumerates reduced words in the standard Magnus automorphisms. It represents their action by exact reduced free words, identifies equal automorphisms, and records differences of class-two signatures. Thus every column it outputs is an actual relation in R_n; completeness of the enumeration is unnecessary.

For n=3, radii<=5 give a primitive rank15 relation lattice. `agent2_gl_closure.py` closes it under elementary GL3 actions and obtains primitive rank18 in Q of rank36. It verifies B kills it and rank(B)=18. Hence R_3=ker B.

For n=4, radius<=3 gives primitive rank102. GL4 closure has ranks190,206,206 in Q of rank276. Smith invariants all206 ones; exact rank(B)=70. Hence R_4=ker B.

`agent2_sparse_closure.py` embeds the rank4 relation basis on all four-element subsets and adds all pairs of Magnus automorphisms that commute (commuting is checked by exact free-word multiplication). It closes under elementary GL_n actions using integral unit-pivot elimination. Only reductions with pivot+/-1 are accepted; discarded candidates cannot invalidate the certificate. Every accepted row is therefore an actual group relation. Pivot ones imply its span is primitive. For n=5 it gets rank1040 and verifies Johnson rank185, sum1225=rank Q. For n=6 it gets rank3606 and verifies Johnson rank399, sum4005=rank Q. It directly checks B kills every source-relation basis row. Thus R_n=ker B for 3<=n<=6. For n=2 this follows from IA2=Inn(F2).

All files are self-contained except imports of our own helpers and SymPy. Runtime: n3 enumeration6sec; n3 closure<1sec; n4 enumeration/closure9sec; n5/6 sparse closure+verification2sec.

## General locality lemma: ker B is generated on at most six indices

Define the Morita trace t:T->S^2 H by embedding L3 in H^{tensor3}, contracting the dual root with the first tensor slot, and symmetrizing the remaining pair. For a root dual e_r^*,
 t(e_r^* tensor [[Xa,Xb],Xc])=delta(r,a) Xb Xc-delta(r,b) Xa Xc.
This formula shows directly tB=0. It also shows t is onto for n>=2: for XaXb choose r!=a and use e_r^* tensor [[Xr,Xa],Xb].

Take the usual integral Hall basis of L3, consisting of left-normed brackets [[Xi,Xj],Xk] with i!=j and the Hall order condition. Each basis vector of T has trace either0 or +/-one monomial XaXb. Therefore ker t has the following integral basis: every zero-trace Hall vector; and, for each monomial XaXb, signed differences between the Hall vectors tracing to that monomial and one chosen representative b0(a,b). Each such kernel-basis vector is supported on at most four indices (a,b and two possible root labels). Every representative b0(a,b) has support contained in {a,b,r0(a,b)}.

In ranks2,3,4 the bracket image equals ker t integrally. This follows by the exact sparse unit-pivot bracket-rank certificates (ranks1,18,70): the accepted primitive image sublattice has the same rational rank as ker t, which is saturated, so they agree. Thus each of the just-described ker t basis elements has a B-preimage using only the labels of that basis element, at most four. Choose one such preimage for each basis element. This gives an integral linear section s:ker t->Q.

Now take a basis vector c of Q, a wedge of two elementary derivations. It uses at most six labels. If Bc!=0, then one derivation's root must equal one of the other's leaves; hence c uses at most five distinct labels. B respects the Z^n weight (leaf counts minus root counts), so Bc is weight homogeneous.

If that weight has a negative coordinate, every Hall term of Bc has trace zero. The chosen s(Bc) is supported on labels already present in c.

If the weight is nonnegative, it is exactly the degree-two monomial XaXb. In the above ker t basis, expansion of Bc uses only Hall vectors already present in Bc together with the single chosen representative b0(a,b). Consequently s(Bc) is supported on the support of c together with the one additional root r0(a,b).

In either case, c-s(Bc) belongs to ker B and uses at most six labels. If Bc=0 the same conclusion holds by taking c itself. Finally, for x=sum_c a_c c in ker B,
 x=sum_c a_c(c-s(Bc)),
because s is linear and sum a_c Bc=0. This proves ker B is generated by the kernels restricted to sets of at most six labels.

Since those local kernels are actual source relations by the finite certificates, ker B=R_n for EVERY n>=2. Equivalently gamma_2(IA_n) intersection A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n); so (n,2) is excluded from D for every n.

## Required final small check
The sparse bracket routine already verifies primitive image ranks for n5,6. Run the same UnitLattice routine on bracket columns for n2,3,4 to explicitly print the primitive image ranks1,18,70 needed in the locality lemma. This is implemented in the next shared checker update.
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_degree2_all_ranks_proof.md').write_text(text)
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_sparse_closure import UnitLattice,johnson_columns
for n in [2,3,4]:
    L=UnitLattice()
    for c in johnson_columns(n):L.insert(c)
    print('Primitive bracket image',n,'rank',len(L.b),'blocked candidates',L.blocked)
    assert len(L.b)==n*n*(n*n-1)//3-n*(n+1)//2

```

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend,inv
from agent2_sparse_closure import UnitLattice
from agent2_gl_closure import gl_action
from itertools import combinations,product
import sympy as S

def magnus3(word,N):
    p={():1}
    for x in word:
        a=abs(x)-1; terms=[((),1),((a,),1)] if x>0 else [(tuple([a]*k),(-1)**k) for k in range(4)]
        q={}
        for u,c in p.items():
            for v,d in terms:
                if len(u)+len(v)<=3:q[u+v]=q.get(u+v,0)+c*d
        p={u:c for u,c in q.items() if c}
    return p

def cubic_sources(depth=4):
    n=3;G=generators(n);N=len(G);off=N*N
    unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));seen={unit:()};layer=[(unit,())];L=UnitLattice(); count=0
    for d in range(1,depth+1):
        nxt=[]
        for A,w in layer:
            for a,g in enumerate(G):
                for s in (1,-1):
                    letter=s*(a+1)
                    if w and w[-1]==-letter:continue
                    B=extend(A,g,s);ww=w+(letter,)
                    if B not in seen:seen[B]=ww;nxt.append((B,ww));continue
                    w0=seen[B]
                    if ww==w0:continue
                    p=magnus3(ww+inv(w0),N)
                    assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p)
                    v={}
                    for u,c in p.items():
                        if len(u)==2:v[u[0]*N+u[1]]=c
                        if len(u)==3:v[off+(u[0]*N+u[1])*N+u[2]]=c
                    L.insert(v);count+=1
        layer=nxt
        print('depth',d,'seen',len(seen),'relations tested',count,'lattice rank',len(L.b),'pure cubic',sum(p>=off for p in L.b),'blocked',L.blocked,flush=True)
    C=UnitLattice()
    for p,v in L.b.items():
        if p>=off:C.insert({i-off:c for i,c in v.items()})
    # Full known quadratic relation lattice gives valid cubic commutator rels.
    H=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_GL_relations.pkl','rb'));P=list(combinations(range(N),2))
    for col in range(H.cols):
        p={}
        for r,(i,j) in enumerate(P):
            c=int(H[r,col])
            if c:p[(i,j)]=c;p[(j,i)]=-c
        for k in range(N):
            v={}
            for (i,j),c in p.items():
                a=(i*N+j)*N+k;b=(k*N+i)*N+j
                v[a]=v.get(a,0)+c;v[b]=v.get(b,0)-c
            C.insert(v)
    print('cubic plus quadratic consequences',len(C.b),'blocked',C.blocked,flush=True)
    return C

def tensor_actions(n=3):
    N=len(generators(n));out=[]
    for a in range(n):
        for b in range(n):
            if a==b:continue
            A=S.eye(n);A[a,b]=1;T=gl_action(n,A);col=[[(i,int(T[i,j])) for i in range(N) if T[i,j]] for j in range(N)]
            act=[]
            for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):
                v={}
                for x,c in col[i]:
                    for y,d in col[j]:
                        for z,e in col[k]:v[(x*N+y)*N+z]=c*d*e
                act.append(v)
            out.append(act)
    return out

def run():
    start=time.time();C=cubic_sources();actions=tensor_actions();idx=0
    from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
    while idx<len(C.queue):
        v=C.queue[idx];idx+=1
        for act in actions:C.insert(apply(act,v))
    print('FINAL CUBIC RELATION RANK',len(C.b),'blocked',C.blocked,'secs',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    pickle.dump(C.b,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_relations.pkl','wb'))
    return C
if __name__=='__main__':run()
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_cubic_source.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_cubic_source.py','exec'))

```

class IntegralLattice:
    def __init__(self):self.b={};self.queue=[];self.blocked=0
    def insert(self,v):
        v={i:int(c) for i,c in v.items() if c}
        changed=False
        while v:
            p=min(v);a=v[p]
            if p not in self.b:
                if a<0:v={i:-c for i,c in v.items()}
                self.b[p]=v;self.queue.append(v);return True
            w=self.b[p];d=w[p];q=a//d
            for i,c in w.items():
                t=v.get(i,0)-q*c
                if t:v[i]=t
                else:v.pop(i,None)
            if v.get(p,0):
                # Euclidean exchange: preserve old pivot row as next remainder.
                self.b[p]=v;self.queue.append(v);v=w;changed=True
        return changed

def magnus3(word,N):

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import compose
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend,inv,cat
n=3;G=generators(n);unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n))
found=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_lifts.pkl','rb'))
targets={}
for a in range(n):
    for b in range(n):
        if a==b:continue
        U=list(unit);Ui=list(unit);U[b]=cat(unit[b],unit[a]);Ui[b]=cat(unit[b],inv(unit[a]));U=tuple(U);Ui=tuple(Ui)
        for k,g in enumerate(G):
            if (a,b,k) not in found:targets[a,b,k]=compose(compose(U,extend(unit,g,1)),Ui)
seen={unit:()};layer=[(unit,())]
for d in range(1,4):
    nxt=[]
    for A,w in layer:
        for a,g in enumerate(G):
            for s in (1,-1):
                letter=s*(a+1)
                if w and w[-1]==-letter:continue
                B=extend(A,g,s);ww=w+(letter,)
                if B not in seen:seen[B]=ww;nxt.append((B,ww))
    layer=nxt
print('ball',len(seen),'targets',len(targets),flush=True)
def evaluate(w):
    A=unit
    for x in w:A=extend(A,G[abs(x)-1],1 if x>0 else -1)
    return A
for A,w in seen.items():
    Ai=evaluate(inv(w))
    for key,target in list(targets.items()):
        B=compose(Ai,target)
        if B in seen:
            word=w+seen[B]
            assert evaluate(word)==target
            found[key]=word;targets.pop(key)
            print('FOUND',key,'length',len(word),'word',word,flush=True)
    if not targets:break
print('remaining',len(targets),flush=True)
pickle.dump(found,open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_lifts_complete.pkl','wb'))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_find_nielsen_lifts.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_find_nielsen_lifts.py','exec'))

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import IntegralLattice,magnus3
from agent2_source_relations import generators
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
from itertools import product,combinations
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ

def actions():
    N=9;off=N*N;lifts=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_lifts_complete.pkl','rb'));out=[]
    for a in range(3):
        for b in range(3):
            if a==b:continue
            ps=[magnus3(lifts[a,b,k],N) for k in range(N)]
            lin=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==1} for p in ps]
            quad=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2} for p in ps]
            act=[]
            for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):
                v={}
                for P,Q in [(lin[i],lin[j]),(quad[i],lin[j]),(lin[i],quad[j])]:
                    for x,c in P.items():
                        for y,d in Q.items():
                            w=x+y;k=w[0]*N+w[1] if len(w)==2 else off+(w[0]*N+w[1])*N+w[2]
                            v[k]=v.get(k,0)+c*d
                act.append({k:c for k,c in v.items() if c})
            for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):
                v={}
                for x,c in lin[i].items():
                    for y,d in lin[j].items():
                        for z,e in lin[k].items():v[off+(x[0]*N+y[0])*N+z[0]]=c*d*e
                act.append(v)
            out.append(act)
    return out

def run():
    start=time.time();B=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_mixed_relations.pkl','rb'));L=IntegralLattice()
    for v in B.values():L.insert(v)
    H=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_GL_relations.pkl','rb'));N=9;off=81;P=list(combinations(range(N),2))
    for col in range(H.cols):
        p={}
        for r,(i,j) in enumerate(P):
            c=int(H[r,col])
            if c:p[(i,j)]=c;p[(j,i)]=-c
        for k in range(N):
            v={}
            for (i,j),c in p.items():
                a=off+(i*N+j)*N+k;b=off+(k*N+i)*N+j
                v[a]=v.get(a,0)+c;v[b]=v.get(b,0)-c
            L.insert(v)
    acts=actions();idx=0
    while idx<len(L.queue):
        v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
        for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
    C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
    print('Mixed relation rank',len(L.b),'pure cubic rank',len(C),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
    M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
    for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
        for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
    D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
    from collections import Counter
    print('SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),flush=True)
    assert len(C)==197 and all(d==1 for d in ds)
    pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_primitive_relations.pkl','wb'))
    return C
if __name__=='__main__':run()
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_cubic_nonlinear_closure.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_cubic_nonlinear_closure.py','exec'))

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import compose
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend,inv
n=3;G=generators(n);unit=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));U=list(unit);U[0]=(-1,);U=tuple(U)
targets={k:compose(compose(U,extend(unit,g,1)),U) for k,g in enumerate(G)};found={}
seen={unit:()};layer=[(unit,())]
for d in range(1,5):
    nxt=[]
    for A,w in layer:
        for a,g in enumerate(G):
            for s in (1,-1):
                letter=s*(a+1)
                if w and w[-1]==-letter:continue
                B=extend(A,g,s);ww=w+(letter,)
                if B not in seen:
                    seen[B]=ww;nxt.append((B,ww))
                    for key,target in list(targets.items()):
                        if B==target:found[key]=ww;targets.pop(key)
    layer=nxt
print('ball',len(seen),'targets',len(targets),flush=True)
def evaluate(w):
    A=unit
    for x in w:A=extend(A,G[abs(x)-1],1 if x>0 else -1)
    return A
for A,w in seen.items():
    if not targets:break
    Ai=evaluate(inv(w))
    for key,target in list(targets.items()):
        B=compose(Ai,target)
        if B in seen:
            word=w+seen[B];assert evaluate(word)==target
            found[key]=word;targets.pop(key);print('FOUND',key,'length',len(word),flush=True)
print('remaining',len(targets),'lifts',found,flush=True)
pickle.dump(found,open('workspace/agent2_inversion_lifts.pkl','wb'))
'''

sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import IntegralLattice,magnus3,compose
from agent2_cubic_nonlinear_closure import actions

from itertools import combinations
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from collections import Counter
N=9;off=81;G=generators(3);unit=((1,),(2,),(3,));start=time.time()
seen={unit:()};layer=[(unit,())]
for d in range(1,4):
    nxt=[]
    for A,w in layer:
        for a,g in enumerate(G):
            B=extend(A,g,1);ww=w+(a+1,)
            if B not in seen:seen[B]=ww;nxt.append((B,ww))
    layer=nxt
print('positive ball3 size',len(seen),flush=True)
L=IntegralLattice()
for v in pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_mixed_relations.pkl','rb')).values():L.insert(v)
for v in pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_relations.pkl','rb')).values():L.insert({i+off:c for i,c in v.items()})
count=0;added=0
for (A,u),(B,v) in combinations(seen.items(),2):
    if compose(A,B)!=compose(B,A):continue
    count+=1;p=magnus3(u+v+inv(u)+inv(v),N);vec={}
    for w,c in p.items():
        if len(w)==2:vec[w[0]*N+w[1]]=c
        elif len(w)==3:vec[off+(w[0]*N+w[1])*N+w[2]]=c
    if L.insert(vec):added+=1
print('commuting pairs',count,'added',added,'rank',len(L.b),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
acts=actions();idx=0
while idx<len(L.queue):
    v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
    for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
    for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
print('FINAL cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
pickle.dump(L.b,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_mixed_plus_commuting.pkl','wb'))
'''

############################################################################
##
#M IA group
##
############################################################################
InstallMethod( EmbeddingOfIASubgroup, "for a free group automorphism group",
        [ IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
        function(A)
# conventions on generators:
# M[i][j][k] is M_{x_i,[x_j,x_k]} if all variables i,j,k are <= n. x_i^-1 is represented as i+n.
# M[i][j][k] is a variable only if j<k. If j>k, it is stored as M[i][k][j]^-1.
# C[i][j] is C_{x_i,x_j} if i,j <= n. If j>n, then it is C_{x_i,x_{j-n}}^-1.
#
# Beware that GAP composes maps left-to-right, while the article composes maps right-to-left.
# GAP's commutator is [g,h] = g^-1h^-1gh, while the article's commutator is ghg^-1h^-1.
# Therefore, in converting an article formula to a GAP formula, multiplication order must be reversed,
# and commutators must be switched from [g,h] to [h,g].
#
    local n, F, G, C, M, i, p, q, r, s, t, u, v, rels, endos, Gendos, epi, alpha;
    
    G := Source(One(A)); # the free group
    n := RankOfFreeGroup(G);
    
    C := List(Arrangements([1..n],2),p->Concatenation("C(",String(p[1]),",",String(p[2]),")"));
    M := [];
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p[2]>p[3] then continue; fi;
        Add(M,Concatenation("M(",String(p[1]),",[",String(p[2]),",",String(p[3]),"])"));
    od;
    F := FreeGroup(Concatenation(C,M));
    i := 1;
    epi := [];
    C := List([1..n],i->[]);
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        C[p[1]][p[2]] := F.(i);
        C[p[1]][p[2]+n] := F.(i)^-1;
        q := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        q[p[1]] := q[p[2]]*q[p[1]]/q[p[2]];
        Add(epi,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,q));
        i := i+1;
    od;
    M := List([1..2*n],i->List([1..2*n],j->[]));
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p[2]>p[3] then continue; fi;
        M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]] := F.(i);
        M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]] := F.(i)^-1;
        q := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
        q[p[1]] := Comm(q[p[2]]^-1,q[p[3]]^-1)*q[p[1]];
        Add(epi,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,q));
        i := i+1;
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]+n] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]+n] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]+n][p[2]+n] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]+n]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]+n][p[3]+n] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]+n]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
            
        M[p[1]][p[3]+n][p[2]] := M[p[1]][p[2]][p[3]]^C[p[1]][p[3]];
        M[p[1]][p[2]+n][p[3]] := M[p[1]][p[3]][p[2]]^C[p[1]][p[2]];
        
        for s in [0,n] do for t in [0,n] do
            M[p[1]+n][p[2]+s][p[3]+t] := Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s]^-1,C[p[1]][p[3]+t]^-1) / M[p[1]][p[2]+s][p[3]+t];            
            M[p[1]+n][p[3]+s][p[2]+t] := Comm(C[p[1]][p[3]+s]^-1,C[p[1]][p[2]+t]^-1) / M[p[1]][p[3]+s][p[2]+t];            
        od; od;
    od;
    
    rels := [];
    
    # R0: M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]) * M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_b^beta])
    # not necessary anymore, since we put only half the generators
    if true then
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            Add(rels,M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]*M[p[1]+s[1]][p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+s[2]]);
        od;
    od;
    fi;
    
    # R1: [C(x_a,x_b),C(x_c,x_d)]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        if p<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            if p[1]<>q[1] and p[1]<>q[2] and p[2]<>q[1] then
                Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]],C[q[1]][q[2]]));
            fi;
        od;
    od;
    
    # R2: [M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]),M(x_d^delta,[x_e^epsilon,x_f^zeta])]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for t in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] then continue; fi;
            if p[1]+s[1]<>q[1]+t[1] and not p[1] in q{[2,3]} and not q[1] in p{[2,3]} then
                Add(rels,Comm(M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]],M[q[1]+t[1]][q[2]+t[2]][q[3]+t[3]]));
            fi;    
        od; od;
    od; od;
    
    # R3: [C(x_a,x_b),M(x_c^gamma,[x_d^delta,x_e^epsilon])]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        if p<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for t in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if not p[1] in q and not q[1] in p then
                Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]],M[q[1]+t[1]][q[2]+t[2]][q[3]+t[3]]));
            fi;
        od; od;
    od;
    
    # R4: [C(x_c,x_b)*C(x_a,x_b),C(x_c,x_a)]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,Comm(C[p[3]][p[2]]*C[p[1]][p[2]],C[p[3]][p[1]]));
    od;
    
    # R5: M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])^C(x_a,x_b^beta) / M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_b^-beta])
    # not necessary anymore, since we put only a quarter of the generators
    if true then
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]^C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]/M[p[1]+s[1]][p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+n-s[2]]);
    od; od;
    fi;

    # R6: M(x_a^-alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])*M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma]) / [C(x_a,x_c^gamma)^-1,C(x_a,x_b^beta)^-1]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[1]+n-s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]*M[p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]]/Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^-1,C[p[1]][p[3]+s[3]]^-1));
    od; od;
    
    # R7: [C(x_a,x_b^beta)^-1,M(x_a^-alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])] / [C(x_a,x_d^delta)^-1,C(x_a,x_c^gamma)^-1]
    for p in Arrangements([1..n],4) do for s in Tuples([0,n],4) do
        if p{[1,2]}<>[1,2] then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,Comm(C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^-1,M[p[2]+s[2]][p[3]+s[3]][p[4]+s[4]]^-1)/Comm(C[p[1]][p[3]+s[3]]^-1,C[p[1]][p[4]+s[4]]^-1)
... (1,096 characters cut) ...
1,2] then continue; fi;
        if p[4] in p{[1,3]} then continue; fi;
        Add(rels,M[p[3]+s[3]][p[2]+s[2]][p[1]+s[1]]*M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[4]+s[4]]*
            M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[2]+s[2]]^C[p[3]][p[4]+n-s[4]]/M[p[3]+s[3]][p[1]+s[1]][p[4]+s[4]]^C[p[1]][p[2]+s[2]]);
    od; od; od;
    
    endos := [];
    Gendos := [];
    # I_1
    t := []; u := [];
    for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
        Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
        if 1=q[2] then Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]^-1); else Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]); fi;
    od;
    for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
        if q[2]>q[3] or s<>[0,0,0] then continue; fi;
        Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
        if 1 in q then s[Position(q,1)] := n-s[Position(q,1)]; fi;
        Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
    od; od;
    Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));
    t := ShallowCopy(GeneratorsOfGroup(G));
    t[1] := t[1]^-1;
    Add(Gendos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,t));
    
    # P_s for all permutations s on {1..n}
    for p in GeneratorsOfGroup(SymmetricGroup(n)) do
        t := []; u := [];
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            Add(u,C[q[1]^p][q[2]^p]);
        od;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] then continue; fi;
            Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            Add(u,M[q[1]^p+s[1]][q[2]^p+s[2]][q[3]^p+s[3]]);
        od; od;
        Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));
        Add(Gendos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(G,Permuted(GeneratorsOfGroup(G),p^-1)));
    od;
    
    # M_{x_1^alpha,x_2^beta}
    for p in Tuples([0,n],2) do
        if p[1]=0 then alpha := 1; else alpha := -1; fi;
        t := []; u := [];
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],2) do
            Add(t,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            if q[2]=1 and q[1]<>2 then # C(x_c,x_a)
                Add(u,(C[q[1]][2+p[2]]*C[q[1]][1+p[1]])^alpha);
            elif q[1]=1 and q[2]<>2 then # C(x_a,x_c)
                Add(u,M[1+p[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[2]]*C[1][q[2]]);
            elif q[1]=2 and q[2]<>1 then # C(x_b,x_c)
                Add(u,M[1+p[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[2]+n]*C[2][q[2]]);
            elif q=[2,1] then # C(x_b,x_a)
                Add(u,(C[2][1+p[1]]*C[1][2+p[2]])^alpha);
            else
                Add(u,C[q[1]][q[2]]);
            fi;
        od;
        for q in Arrangements([1..n],3) do for s in Tuples([0,n],3) do
            if q[2]>q[3] or s<>[0,0,0] then continue; fi;
            Add(t,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            if q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[q[1]][2+n-p[2]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][2+p[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] and not 2 in q then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^-alpha,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][2+n-p[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^(C[q[1]][1]^alpha)*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and not 1 in q then # M(x_b^beta,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,(M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]*M[1+n-p[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]])^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and not 1 in q then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_c^gamma,x_d^delta])
                Add(u,M[2+n-p[2]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]*M[1+p[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and q[2]+s[2]=2+p[2] then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^beta,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=1+p[1] and q[2]+s[2]=2+n-p[2] then # M(x_a^alpha,[x_b^-beta,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] then # M(x_b^beta,[x_a^alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+n-s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+n-s[3]]*C[2][q[3]+n-s[3]]*M[q[2]+s[2]][q[1]+n-s[1]][q[3]+s[3]]*C[1][q[3]+s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] then # M(x_b^beta,[x_a^-alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,(C[1][q[3]+n-s[3]]*M[1+p[1]][q[3]+s[3]][2+n-p[2]]*C[2][q[3]+s[3]]*M[2+n-p[2]][q[3]+n-s[3]][1+p[1]])^(C[q[3]][1+n-p[1]]*C[q[3]][2+n-p[2]]));
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_a^alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,((C[2][q[3]+s[3]]*M[q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]][q[1]+s[1]])^C[q[3]][2+p[2]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[3]+s[3]][q[2]+n-s[2]])^C[q[3]][1+p[1]]*C[1][q[3]+n-s[3]]);
            elif q[1]+s[1]=2+n-p[2] and q[2]+s[2]=1+n-p[1] then # M(x_b^-beta,[x_a^-alpha,x_c^gamma])
                Add(u,(C[1][q[3]+s[3]]^C[q[3]][1+n-p[1]]*M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]])^C[q[3]][2+n-p[2]]*M[1+p[1]][q[1]+s[1]][q[3]+s[3]]*C[2][q[3]+n-s[3]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and q[3]+s[3]=2+p[2] then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_b^beta])
                Add(u,M[q[1]+s[1]][q[2]+s[2]][q[3]+s[3]]^C[1][2+p[2]]);
            elif q[2]+s[2]=1+p[1] and q[3]+s[3]=2+n-p[2] then # M(x_c^gamma,[x_a^alpha,x_b^-beta])

        od; od;
        Add(endos,GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,t,u));

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators,red,inv,extend,cat
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3,IntegralLattice
from agent2_cubic_nonlinear_closure import actions
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
from itertools import permutations,product
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from collections import Counter

def gapcomm(a,b):return cat(inv(a),inv(b),a,b)
def power(a,b):return cat(inv(b),a,b)
def div(a,b):return cat(a,inv(b))
def seeds():
    n=3;C={};M={};G=generators(n);gi={g:i+1 for i,g in enumerate(G)};nextidx=1;conv={}
    for i,j in permutations(range(n),2):
        C[i,j]=(nextidx,);C[i,j+n]=(-nextidx,);conv[nextidx]=(gi['C',i,j],);nextidx+=1
    for i,j,k in permutations(range(n),3):
        if j>k:continue
        M[i,j,k]=(nextidx,);M[i,k,j]=(-nextidx,)
        a=gi['C',i,j];b=gi['C',i,k];c=gi['M',i,j,k]
        conv[nextidx]=(-b,-a,b,a,c);nextidx+=1
        M[i,k,j+n]=power(M[i,j,k],C[i,j])
        M[i,j,k+n]=power(M[i,k,j],C[i,k])
        M[i,k+n,j+n]=power(M[i,j,k+n],C[i,j])
        M[i,j+n,k+n]=power(M[i,k,j+n],C[i,k])
        M[i,k+n,j]=power(M[i,j,k],C[i,k])
        M[i,j+n,k]=power(M[i,k,j],C[i,j])
        for s,t in product((0,n),repeat=2):
            M[i+n,j+s,k+t]=div(gapcomm(inv(C[i,j+s]),inv(C[i,k+t])),M[i,j+s,k+t])
            M[i+n,k+s,j+t]=div(gapcomm(inv(C[i,k+s]),inv(C[i,j+t])),M[i,k+s,j+t])
    R=[]
    for p in permutations(range(n),3):
        a,b,c=p
        for sa,sb,sc in product((0,n),repeat=3):R.append(cat(M[a+sa,b+sb,c+sc],M[a+sa,c+sc,b+sb]))
    R.append(gapcomm(C[0,1],C[2,1]))
    a,b,c=0,1,2
    R.append(gapcomm(cat(C[c,b],C[a,b]),C[c,a]))
    for sa,sb,sc in product((0,n),repeat=3):
        R.append(div(power(M[a+sa,b+sb,c+sc],C[a,b+sb]),M[a+sa,c+sc,b+n-sb]))
        R.append(div(cat(M[a+n-sa,b+sb,c+sc],M[a+sa,b+sb,c+sc]),gapcomm(inv(C[a,c+sc]),inv(C[a,b+sb]))))
    for sa,sb,sc,sd in product((0,n),repeat=4):
        d=b
        R.append(div(cat(M[c+sc,b+sb,a+sa],M[c+sc,a+sa,d+sd],power(M[c+sc,a+sa,b+sb],C[c,d+n-sd])),power(M[c+sc,a+sa,d+sd],C[a,b+sb])))
    out=[]
    unit=((1,),(2,),(3,))
    for r in R:
        # Reverse GAP order, then replace package left-M by exact right-M words.
        word=cat(*(conv[x] if x>0 else inv(conv[-x]) for x in r[::-1]))
        A=unit
        for x in word:A=extend(A,G[abs(x)-1],1 if x>0 else -1)
        assert A==unit,(r,word,A)
        out.append(word)
    print('Verified exact Day-Putman seed relators',len(out),'maxlength',max(map(len,out)),flush=True)
    return out

def run():
    N=9;off=81;L=IntegralLattice()
    for v in pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_mixed_relations.pkl','rb')).values():L.insert(v)
    for v in pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_relations.pkl','rb')).values():L.insert({i+off:c for i,c in v.items()})
    for word in seeds():
        p=magnus3(word,N);vec={}
        assert not any(len(w)==1 for w in p)
        for w,c in p.items():
            if len(w)==2:vec[w[0]*N+w[1]]=c
            elif len(w)==3:vec[off+(w[0]*N+w[1])*N+w[2]]=c
        L.insert(vec)
    acts=actions();idx=0
    while idx<len(L.queue):
        v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
        for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
    C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
    Mat=S.zeros(729,len(C))
    for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
        for i,c in v.items():Mat[i,j]=c
    D=smith_normal_form(Mat,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
    print('Cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),flush=True)
    assert len(C)==197 and all(d==1 for d in ds)
    pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_ia3_cubic_primitive_relations.pkl','wb'))
if __name__=='__main__':run()
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_dayputman_cubic.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_dayputman_cubic.py','exec'))

class UnitLattice:
 def __init__(self):self.b={};self.blocked=0;self.pending=[]
 def insert(self,v):
  v=v.copy()
  while v:
   p=min(v);a=v[p]
   if p not in self.b:
    self.b[p]={k:x*(1 if a>0 else -1) for k,x in v.items()};return True
   w=self.b[p];d=w[p]
   if a%d==0:v=combine(v,1,w,-a//d)
   else:
    g,s,t=bezout(d,a)
    self.b[p]=combine(w,s,v,t)
    v=combine(v,d//g,w,-a//g)
  return False

def image(n,d):
 g=generators(n);bs=g
 for k in range(2,d+1):
  L=UnitLattice()
  for a in g:
   for b in bs:L.insert(bracket(a,b))
  old=-1
  while old!=len(L.b):
   old=len(L.b);todo=L.pending;L.pending=[]
   for a in todo:L.insert(a)
  bs=list(L.b.values())
  if k==2:expected=n*n*(n*n-1)//3-n*(n+1)//2
  elif k==3:expected=n*(n**4-n*n)//4-(n**3+2*n)//3
  else:expected=None
  if expected is not None:print("expected",expected,"accepted",len(bs),"blocked",L.blocked,flush=True)
  print('integral image',n,k,len(bs),'pivot sizes',sorted(set(v[p] for p,v in L.b.items())),flush=True)
 return bs
if __name__=='__main__':
 for n in range(2,7):image(n,3)

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import json,sys,pickle,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3,IntegralLattice
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
from itertools import product
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from collections import Counter
N=9;off=81;data=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_gap_ia3_data.json'))
def vector(p):
    v={}
    for u,c in p.items():
        if len(u)==2:v[u[0]*N+u[1]]=c
        if len(u)==3:v[off+(u[0]*N+u[1])*N+u[2]]=c
    return v

def action(ws):
    ps=[magnus3(tuple(w[::-1]),N) for w in ws]
    lin=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==1} for p in ps]
    quad=[{u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2} for p in ps]
    act=[]
    for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):
        p={}
        for P,Q in [(lin[i],lin[j]),(quad[i],lin[j]),(lin[i],quad[j])]:
            for x,c in P.items():
                for y,d in Q.items():p[x+y]=p.get(x+y,0)+c*d
        act.append(vector(p))
    for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):
        p={}
        for x,c in lin[i].items():
            for y,d in lin[j].items():
                for z,e in lin[k].items():p[x+y+z]=p.get(x+y+z,0)+c*d*e
        act.append(vector(p))
    return act

def comm(v,k):
    ans={}
    for i,c in v.items():
        if i>=off:continue
        a,b=divmod(i,N);j=off+(a*N+b)*N+k;l=off+(k*N+a)*N+b
        ans[j]=ans.get(j,0)+c;ans[l]=ans.get(l,0)-c
    return ans
start=time.time();L=IntegralLattice()
for r in data['rels']:
    p=magnus3(tuple(r['word'][::-1]),N)
    assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p)
    L.insert(vector(p))
acts=[action(e['images']) for e in data['endomorphisms']];idx=0
while idx<len(L.queue):
    v=L.queue[idx];idx+=1
    for k in range(N):L.insert(comm(v,k))
    for act in acts:L.insert(apply(act,v))
for iteration in range(10):
    changed=0
    for v in list(L.b.values()):
        for k in range(N):changed+=L.insert(comm(v,k))
        for act in acts:changed+=L.insert(apply(act,v))
    print('stability',iteration,'changes',changed,flush=True)
    if not changed:break
C={p-off:{i-off:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in L.b.items() if p>=off}
M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
    for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
print('Direct LPRES mixed rank',len(L.b),'pure cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),'secs',time.time()-start,flush=True)
pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_direct_lpres_cubic.pkl','wb'))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_direct_lpres_cubic.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_direct_lpres_cubic.py','exec'))

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,json
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import generators,extend,cat,inv
from agent2_cubic_source import compose
D=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_gap_ia3_data.json'));G=generators(3);gi={g:i+1 for i,g in enumerate(G)};unit=((1,),(2,),(3,));conv={}
for k,g in enumerate(D['names'],1):
    typ,*a=g;a=[x-1 for x in a]
    if typ=='C':conv[k]=(gi[(typ,*a)],)
    else:
        i,j,l=a;b=gi['C',i,j];c=gi['C',i,l];m=gi['M',i,j,l];conv[k]=(-c,-b,c,b,m)
def evaluate(w):
    A=unit
    for x in w:A=extend(A,G[abs(x)-1],1 if x>0 else -1)
    return A
def convert(w):return cat(*(conv[x] if x>0 else inv(conv[-x]) for x in w[::-1]))
base=[evaluate(conv[k]) for k in range(1,10)]
for e in D['endomorphisms']:
    name=e['name']
    if name=='I1':S=((-1,),(2,),(3,));Si=S
    elif name.startswith('M12'):
        sa,sb=map(int,name[4:-1].split(','));alpha=1 if sa==0 else -1;beta=1 if sb==0 else -1
        # (xb^beta * xa^alpha)^alpha
        w=(beta*2,alpha*1);w=w if alpha==1 else inv(w)
        S=(w,(2,),(3,))
        wi=((-beta*2,1) if alpha==1 else (1,beta*2));Si=(wi,(2,),(3,))
        assert compose(S,Si)==unit
    else:continue
    flags=[True,True];bad=[]
    for k,A in enumerate(base):
        B=evaluate(convert(e['images'][k]))
        f=B==compose(compose(S,A),Si);g=B==compose(compose(Si,A),S)
        flags[0]&=f;flags[1]&=g
        if not(f or g):bad.append(k+1)
    print(name,'consistent conjugation directions',flags,'bad gens',bad,flush=True)
'''

import agent2_direct_lpres_cubic as d

from agent2_sparse_closure import apply

from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ

G=generators(3);gi={g:i+1 for i,g in enumerate(G)};unit=((1,),(2,),(3,));conv={}
for k,g in enumerate(d.data['names'],1):
    typ,*a=g;a=[x-1 for x in a]
    if typ=='C':conv[k]=(gi[(typ,*a)],)
    else:
        i,j,l=a;b=gi['C',i,j];c=gi['C',i,l];m=gi['M',i,j,l];conv[k]=(-c,-b,c,b,m)
def evaluate(w):
    word=cat(*(conv[x] if x>0 else inv(conv[-x]) for x in w));A=unit
    for x in word:A=extend(A,G[abs(x)-1],1 if x>0 else -1)
    return A
thetas={e['name']:[tuple(w[::-1]) for w in e['images']] for e in d.data['endomorphisms']}
def substitute(theta,w):return cat(*(theta[x-1] if x>0 else inv(theta[-x-1]) for x in w))
rels=[]
for a,b in [('I1','I1'),('M12(0, 0)','M12(0, 3)'),('M12(0, 3)','M12(0, 0)'),('M12(3, 0)','M12(3, 3)'),('M12(3, 3)','M12(3, 0)')]:
    for k in range(9):
        word=cat(substitute(thetas[a],thetas[b][k]),(-k-1,))
        assert evaluate(word)==unit,(a,b,k)
        rels.append(word);p=magnus3(word,9);assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p);d.L.insert(d.vector(p))
print('Verified consistency identities',len(rels),'maxlen',max(map(len,rels)),flush=True)
idx=0
while idx<len(d.L.queue):
    v=d.L.queue[idx];idx+=1
    for k in range(9):d.L.insert(d.comm(v,k))
    for act in d.acts:d.L.insert(apply(act,v))
C={p-81:{i-81:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in d.L.b.items() if p>=81};M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
    for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
D=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(D[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
print('CONSISTENCY SOURCE cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),flush=True)
pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_consistency_cubic.pkl','wb'))

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''import sys,json,time,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent2_direct_lpres_cubic as d
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import product
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3
from agent2_sparse_closure import apply
import sympy as S
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from sympy.polys.domains import ZZ
from collections import Counter
N=9;data=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_aut_action_loops.json'));start=time.time()
def mul(a,b):
    v={}
    for x,c in a.items():
        for y,e in b.items():
            if len(x)+len(y)<=3:v[x+y]=v.get(x+y,0)+c*e
    return {x:c for x,c in v.items() if c}
actions=[]
for e in d.data['endomorphisms']:
    qs=[{u:c for u,c in magnus3(tuple(w[::-1]),N).items() if u} for w in e['images']]
    A={}
    for i in range(N):A[(i,)]=qs[i]
    for i,j in product(range(N),repeat=2):A[i,j]=mul(qs[i],qs[j])
    for i,j,k in product(range(N),repeat=3):A[i,j,k]=mul(A[i,j],qs[k])
    actions.append(A)
def transform(A,p):
    out={}
    for u,c in p.items():
        for v,e in A[u].items():out[v]=out.get(v,0)+c*e
    return {v:c for v,c in out.items() if c}
@lru_cache(None)
def image(word):
    if not word:return tuple({(i,):1} for i in range(N))
    A=actions[word[-1]]
    return tuple(transform(A,p) for p in image(word[:-1]))
added=0
for idx,(wa,wb) in enumerate(data['loops']):
    pa=image(tuple(wa));pb=image(tuple(wb))
    for k in range(N):
        e={u:c for u,c in pa[k].items() if len(u)==1}
        assert e=={u:c for u,c in pb[k].items() if len(u)==1}
        p={u:pa[k].get(u,0)-pb[k].get(u,0) for u in pa[k].keys()|pb[k].keys()}
        p2={u:c for u,c in p.items() if len(u)==2 and c}
        for u,c in p2.items():
            for v,a in e.items():p[u+v]=p.get(u+v,0)-c*a
        if d.L.insert(d.vector(p)):added+=1
    if idx%500==0:print('loops',idx,'added',added,'cache',image.cache_info(),'secs',time.time()-start,flush=True)
print('Loops added',added,flush=True)
idx=0
while idx<len(d.L.queue):
    v=d.L.queue[idx];idx+=1
    for k in range(N):d.L.insert(d.comm(v,k))
    for act in d.acts:d.L.insert(apply(act,v))
C={p-81:{i-81:c for i,c in v.items()} for p,v in d.L.b.items() if p>=81};M=S.zeros(729,len(C))
for j,v in enumerate(C.values()):
    for i,c in v.items():M[i,j]=c
Diag=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ);ds=[abs(Diag[i,i]) for i in range(len(C))]
print('ALL-LOOP SOURCE cubic rank',len(C),'SNF',dict(Counter(ds)),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
pickle.dump(C,open('workspace/agent2_all_loop_cubic.pkl','wb'))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_all_loop_cubic.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_all_loop_cubic.py','exec'))

from pathlib import Path
code=r'''"""Fast field-membership tests for current VERIFIED rank3 source relation lattice.
Source generators: C12,C13,C21,C23,C31,C32,M1;23,M2;13,M3;12.
M is LEFT multiplication xi -> [xj,xk]xi. Word products R-to-L composition.
Each returned nonzero residue certifies an additional source relation is new
IF supplied word has independently been checked an actual automorphism identity.
This is NOT an IA presentation or a classification theorem.
"""
import pickle,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cubic_source import magnus3

def insert(B,v,p):
    v={k:int(c)%p for k,c in v.items() if c%p}
    while v:
        k=min(v);c=v[k]
        if k in B:
            for j,d in B[k].items():
                a=(v.get(j,0)-c*d)%p
                if a:v[j]=a
                else:v.pop(j,None)
        else:
            a=pow(c,-1,p);B[k]={j:a*d%p for j,d in v.items()};return True
    return False

def reduce(B,v,p):
    v={k:int(c)%p for k,c in v.items() if c%p}
    # Keep nonpivot terms; cannot stop at the first such term.
    for k,w in sorted(B.items()):
        c=v.get(k,0)
        if c:
            for j,d in w.items():
                a=(v.get(j,0)-c*d)%p
                if a:v[j]=a
                else:v.pop(j,None)
    return v

def vector(word):
    p=magnus3(tuple(word),9);v={}
    assert not any(len(u)==1 for u in p),'identity word must have zero exponent sums'
    for u,c in p.items():
        if len(u)==2:v[9*u[0]+u[1]]=c
        if len(u)==3:v[81+81*u[0]+9*u[1]+u[2]]=c
    return v

def load():return pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_source_defect_bases.pkl','rb'))
def defect(word,bases=None):
    if bases is None:bases=load()
    v=vector(word)
    return {p:reduce(B,v,p) for p,B in bases.items()}
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_source_defect.py').write_text(code)
import sys,pickle
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent2_direct_lpres_cubic as d
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_source_defect.py','exec'))

ball=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_left_ball5.pkl','rb'));print('Loaded',len(ball),flush=True)
cache={};adj=[[] for n in nodes];available=0;missing=0
for ia,j,ib in edges:
    Ma=nodes[ia][2];Mb=nodes[ib][2];key=(Ma,j)
    if key not in cache:
        Ta=nodes[sections[Ma]][0];Tbi=nodes[sections[Mb]][1];h=compose(compose(Ta,S[j]),Tbi)
        cache[key]=ball.get(h)
    w=cache[key]
    if w is None:missing+=1;continue
    available+=1;adj[ia].append((ib,w));adj[ib].append((ia,inv(w)))
print('RSavailable',available,'missing',missing,'unique factors',len(cache),flush=True)
RW={i:() for i in sections.values()};q=deque(RW);bases=load();new=[];tested=0
while q:
    a=q.popleft();u=RW[a]
    for b,w in adj[a]:
        cand=cat(u,w)
        if b not in RW:RW[b]=cand;q.append(b);continue
        rel=cat(cand,inv(RW[b]))
        if not rel:continue
        tested+=1;v=vector(rel);res={p:reduce(B,v,p) for p,B in bases.items()}
        if any(res.values()):
            new.append(list(rel))
            for p,B in bases.items():insert(B,v,p)
            print('NEWactualRSrel',len(new),'length',len(rel),'modranks',{p:len(B) for p,B in bases.items()},flush=True)
print('RSFINAL','rewritten nodes',len(RW),'tested',tested,'new',len(new),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
json.dump({'relators':new,'source_order':D['names'],'composition':'RTL','left_M':True},open('workspace/agent2_RS_new_relators.json','w'))
'''
Path('workspace/agent2_RS_prototype.py').write_text(code)
exec(compile(code,'workspace/agent2_RS_prototype.py','exec'))

#############################################################################
##
#W  AutGrp.gi                FGA package                    Christian Sievers
##
##  Methods for automorphism groups of free groups
##
#Y  2003 - 2012
##


#############################################################################
##
#M  AutomorphismGroup( <group> )
##
InstallMethod( AutomorphismGroup,
    "for free groups",
    [ CanComputeWithInverseAutomaton ],
    function( G )
    local n, aut;

    n := RankOfFreeGroup( G );

    if n = 0 then

        aut := AsGroup( [ IdentityMapping ( G ) ] );

    elif n = 1 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO( G ) );
        SetSize( aut, 2 );

    elif n = 2 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorO( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorP( G ), 
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU( G ) );

    elif n = 3 then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorT( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorU( G ) );

    elif IsEvenInt( n ) then

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorQ( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR( G ) );

    else  #  n > 3, odd

        aut := Group( FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorS( G ),
                      FreeGroupAutomorphismsGeneratorR( G ) );

    fi;

    SetIsFinite( aut, n <= 1 );
    SetFilterObj( aut, IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup );

    return aut;

    end );

#############################################################################
##
#M  \in( <hom>, <autgrp> )
##
## tests whether <hom> is in <autgrp>.
##
## Code contributed by Max Horn.
 
InstallMethod( \in,
    "for automorphism groups of free groups",
    [ IsGroupGeneralMapping, IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
    function( hom, aut )
        local G;
        G := AutomorphismDomain( aut );
        return Source( hom ) = G and Range( hom ) = G and IsBijective( hom );
    end );

#############################################################################
##
#M  IsomorphismFpGroup( <group> )
##
##  returns an isomorphism from an automorphism group of a free group
##  to a finitely presented group.
##
##  The presentation follows Bernhard Neumann (see ../doc/manual.bib)
##  Numbers in the comments refer to the equation numbers in that paper.
##
InstallMethod( IsomorphismFpGroup,
    "for automorphism groups of free groups",
    [ IsAutomorphismGroupOfFreeGroup ],
    function( aut )

    local n, f, fp, O, P, U, S, T, Q, R, rels, moreRels,
                    o, p, u, s, t, q, r, iso, isoinv;

    n := RankOfFreeGroup( AutomorphismDomain ( aut ));

    if n = 0 then

        fp := FreeGroup( 0 );

        iso := aut -> One( fp );

    elif n = 1 then

        f := FreeGroup( "O" );
        O := f.1;
        rels := [ O^2 ];  # 6a
        fp := f / rels;

        o := fp.1;
        iso := aut -> o ^ (Order(aut) - 1);

    elif n = 2 then

        f := FreeGroup( "O", "P", "U" );
        O := f.1;  P := f.2;  U := f.3;
        rels := [ P^2                # 5a
                , O^2                # 6a
                , (O*P)^4            # 8a
                , Comm( U, O*U*O )   # 7e
                , (P*O*P*U)^2        # 7i
                , (U*P*O)^3          # 8b
                ];
        fp := f / rels;

        o := fp.1;  p := fp.2;  u := fp.3;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( p, p, o, u); # Q=P

    elif n = 3 then

        f := FreeGroup( "S", "T", "U" );
        S := f.1;  T := f.2;  U := f.3;
        rels := [ (S^5*T^-1)^2                     # 19a
                , T^-1*S*T^2*S^8*T^-1*S*T^2*S^-4   # 19b
                , (S^4*T^-1*S*T^-1)^2              # 19c
                , T^4                              # 19d
                , Comm( U, S^2*T^-1*S*T^-1*S^2 )   # 19e
                , Comm( U, S^-2*T^-1*S*T^-1*U*S^-2*T^-1*S*T^-1) # 19f
        # wrong:  Comm( U, S^2*T^-1*S*T*S*T^-1*U*T*S^-1*T^-1*S^-1*T*S^2 ) # 19g
                , Comm( U, S^-2*T^-1*S*T*S*T^-1*U*
                              T*S^-1*T^-1*S^-1*T*S^2 ) # 19g corrected
                , Comm( U, T^-1*S*T^2*U*T^-1*S*T^2 )   # 19h
                , S^-2*T^-1*S*T^2*S^2*U*S^2*T^-1*S*T^2*S^2*
                  U*S^-2*U*S^2*U^-1*S^-2*U^-1               # 19i
                , (S^-2*T^-1*S*T*U)^2              # 19j
                , (U*T)^3                          # 19k
                ];
        fp := f / rels;

        s := fp.1;  t := fp.2;  u := fp.3;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( t*s^3*(s*t^-1)^2  # 16c
                                 , s^4               # 16a
                                 , s^3*(s*t^-1)^2    # 16b
                                 , u
                                 );

    elif IsEvenInt(n) then

        f := FreeGroup( "Q", "R" );
        Q := f.1;  R := f.2;
        rels := [ (R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1))^2            # 22a
                , (Q*R^3)^(2*(n-1))                # 22d
                , Q^n                              # 22e
                , Comm( (Q*R^3)^(n-1),
                        Q^-1*R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q ) # 22f
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        Q*R^-3*Q^-1*R^-3*Q )       # 22h
                , Comm( R^4, (Q*R^3)^(n-1) )       # 22j
                , Comm( R^4, Q^-2*R^4*Q^2 )        # 22k
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        (Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1) )  # 22l
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*Q^-1*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*Q*R^3 )  # 22m
                , Comm( Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*Q^-1*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^n*Q^-2*R^4*
                          Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^-n*R^3*Q*R^3   )  # 22n
                , (Q*R^3)^-n*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*(Q*R^3)^n*
                    Q^-3*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*Q^-1*R^4*Q^2*R^-3*
                    Q^-1*R^3*Q^-2*R^-4*Q^3*R^3*Q^-2*R^-4*Q^2 # 22o
                , (R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q^-2*R^4*Q^2)^2         # 22p
                , R^12                                       # 22q
                ];
        # finally the relations 22c:
        moreRels := List( [ 2 .. n/2 ],
                          i -> Comm( R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1),
                                     Q^-i*R^3*(Q*R^3)^(n-1)*Q^i ) );
        fp := f / Concatenation(rels, moreRels);

        q := fp.1;  r := fp.2;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( r^3*(q*r^3)^(n-1)   # 21b
                                 , q
                                 , (q*r^3)^(n-1)       # 21a
                                 , q^-2*r^4*q^2*r^-3   # 21c
                                 );

    else  #  n > 3, odd

        f := FreeGroup( "S", "R" );
        S := f.1;  R := f.2;
        rels := [ (R^3*S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1))^2                 # 25a
                , (S^(n+1)*R^3*S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1))^(n-1)*
                    S^((-n)*(n-1))                           # 25b
                , ((S^n)*(S*R^-3)^(n-1))^2                   # 25d
                , Comm( S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1),
                        S^-(n+1)*R^3*S^n*
                          (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(n+1) )           # 25e
                , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^3,
                        S*R^-3*S^(n-1)*R^-3*S )              # 25f
                , Comm( R^4, S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1) )            # 25g
                , Comm( R^4, S^-2*R^4*S^2 )                  # 25h
                , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3,
                        S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*
                          R^-3*S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)         )  # 25i
                , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*S^-(n+1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*
                          R^-3*S^(n+1)*R^3                )  # 25j
# wrong:        , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3,
#                       R^-3*S^-1*R^-3*S*R^3*S^n*
#                         (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^3*
#                         (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^n*R^-3*S^-1*
#                         R^3*S*R^3                       )  # 25k
                , Comm( S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3,
                        R^-3*S^-1*R^-3*S*R^3*S^n*
                          (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-2*R^4*S^2*R^-3*
                          (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^n*R^-3*S^-1*
                          R^3*S*R^3                       )  # 25k corrected

                , R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^-3*R^4*S^2*R^-3*S*
                    R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(n-3)*R^4*S^2*R^-3*
                    S^(n-1)*R^4*S^2*R^-3*S^(n-1)*R^3*S^-2*
                    R^-4*S^(n+3)*R^3*S^-2*R^-4*S^2           # 25l
                , (R^3*(S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(n-2)*R^4*S^2)^2     # 25m
                , R^12                                       # 22q
                ] ;
        # and finally the relations 25c:
        moreRels := List( [ 2 .. (n-1)/2 ],
                          i -> Comm( R^3*S^n*(S*R^-3)^(n-1),
                                     S^(-i*(n+1))*R^3*S^n*
                                     (S*R^-3)^(n-1)*S^(i*(n+1)) ) );
        fp := f / Concatenation( rels, moreRels );

        s := fp.1;  r := fp.2;
        iso := FGA_CurryAutToPQOU( r^3*s^n*(s*r^-3)^(n-1)              # 24c
                                 , s^(n+1)                             # 24a
                                 , s^n*(s*r^-3)^(n-1)                  # 24b
                                 , s^(-2*(n+1))*r^4*s^(2*(n+1))*r^-3   # 24d
                                 );

    fi;

    isoinv := GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( fp, aut,
                                           GeneratorsOfGroup( fp ),
                                           GeneratorsOfGroup( aut ) );

    return GroupHomomorphismByFunction( aut, fp, iso,

sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_source_relations import cat,inv
from agent2_cubic_source import compose
from agent2_source_defect import load,vector,reduce,insert
from collections import deque
D=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_gap_ia3_data.json'));N=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_neumann_relators.json'));unit=((1,),(2,),(3,));S=[]
for e in D['endomorphisms']:
    name=e['name']
    if name=='I1':A=((-1,),(2,),(3,))
    elif name[0]=='P':p=tuple(map(int,name[2:-1].split(',')));A=tuple((i,) for i in p)
    else:
        sa,sb=map(int,name[4:-1].split(','));alpha=1 if sa==0 else -1;beta=1 if sb==0 else -1
        w=(beta*2,alpha);w=w if alpha==1 else inv(w);A=(w,(2,),(3,))
    S.append(A)
Si=[]
for A in S:Si.append(next(B for B in S if compose(A,B)==unit and compose(B,A)==unit))
def matrix(A):return tuple(sum((1 if x==i else -1 if x==-i else 0) for x in A[j]) for i in range(1,4) for j in range(3))
def mm(A,B):return tuple(sum(A[3*i+k]*B[3*k+j] for k in range(3)) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))
SM=list(map(matrix,S));I=matrix(unit);words=[tuple(w[::-1]) for w in N['nielsen_relators']];targets={I}
for w in words:
    M=I;A=unit
    for j in w:M=mm(M,SM[j]);A=compose(A,S[j]);targets.add(M)
    assert A==unit and M==I
sections={I:(unit,unit,())};q=deque([I]);start=time.time()
while not targets<=sections.keys():
    M=q.popleft();A,Ai,w=sections[M]
    for j,B in enumerate(S):
        P=mm(M,SM[j])
        if P not in sections:sections[P]=(compose(A,B),compose(Si[j],Ai),w+(j,));q.append(P)
print('Neumann targets',len(targets),'GLsections',len(sections),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
ball=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_ia3_left_ball5.pkl','rb'));print('ball loaded',flush=True)
one={w[0]:A for A,w in ball.items() if len(w)==1}
def evaluate(w):
    A=unit
    for x in w:A=compose(A,one[x])
    return A
short=[]
for A,w in ball.items():
    if 1<=len(w)<=3:short.append((evaluate(inv(w)),w))
print('MITM prefixes',len(short),flush=True)
factors={};unknown={};new=[];allrels=[];bases=load()
for ridx,w in enumerate(words):
    M=I;out=();bad=[]
    for j in w:
        P=mm(M,SM[j]);key=M,j
        if key not in factors:
            h=compose(compose(sections[M][0],S[j]),sections[P][1]);word=ball.get(h)
            if word is None:
                for Ai,u in short:

    else:print('Olddirect',ridx,'IAwordlen',len(out),flush=True)
print('FINISHED NeumannRS','knownfactors',sum(u is not None for u in factors.values()),'unknown',len(unknown),'newrels',len(new),'time',time.time()-start,flush=True)
json.dump({'new_relators':new,'all_relators':allrels},open('workspace/agent2_neumann_RS_relators.json','w'))
pickle.dump(unknown,open('workspace/agent2_neumann_unknown_factors.pkl','wb'))

import numpy as np,itertools
I=np.eye(4,dtype=int)
def m(A,B):return A@B%2
def inv(A):
 N=(A-I)%2
 return (I+N+N@N+N@N@N)%2
def comm(A,B):return m(m(m(A,B),inv(A)),inv(B))
a=I.copy();a[0,1]=1
b=I.copy();b[1,2]=1
c=I.copy();c[2,3]=1
u=m(b,a);v=m(c,a)
print('a,b,c',a,b,c,sep='\n');print('W',comm(comm(v,u),a),sep='\n')
allA=[]
for bits in itertools.product(range(2),repeat=6):
 A=I.copy()
 for x,(i,j) in zip(bits,[(i,j) for i in range(4) for j in range(i+1,4)]):A[i,j]=x
 if np.array_equal(m(A,A),I):allA.append(A)
for a,b,c in itertools.product(allA,repeat=3):
 u=m(b,a);v=m(c,a);W=comm(comm(v,u),a)
 if not np.array_equal(W,I):
  print('FOUND',a,b,c,W,sep='\n');break
else:print('NO SOLUTION')
